# FYPHub backend orphan audit
Inspection date: 2026-11-11 (Asia/Karachi).
Source baseline: `59215f6f630420a6f962c40d6930d4c5700b5dc3`.
## Disposition
**No backend source file has been confirmed safe to remove. No application file has been moved, deleted, rewritten, or replaced.** Registered APIs without a direct current frontend caller remain protected active routes. Current tests, shared utilities, model registration, migrations, configuration, Docker files, health endpoints, backups and uploaded documents are retained.
This report was created before any possible quarantine action. The confirmed-orphan list is empty, so `backend/_quarantine/` has no files to receive. Permanent deletion is not authorized by the evidence.
## Evidence and method
- 93 frontend TypeScript/JavaScript files parsed using the TypeScript AST; 76 files reachable from `frontend/src/main.tsx`.
- 206 API request/reference sites examined, including inactive source files and dynamic helpers.
- 80 backend application Python files parsed; 79 loaded by the main import graph.
- 268 runtime route registrations inspected (including aliases/framework endpoints).
- Runtime SQLAlchemy metadata, dependency guards, role closures, response models and the actual PostgreSQL schema were inspected read-only.
- Direct and indirect imports include function-local imports, model/schema re-exports and implicit package initializers.
- Full trace data is in [dependency-map.json](docs/backend-audit/dependency-map.json); the unchanged-contract baseline is [before-contract.json](docs/backend-audit/before-contract.json).
## Frontend/authentication review
`main.tsx` mounts `App.tsx`. App resolves the existing opaque cookie session through `/api/v1/auth/me`, uses the same-origin `/api` proxy, and dispatches Admin, Student, Supervisor, Coordinator and Panel Member dashboards. Login/signup/reset remain in App. Portal navigation uses existing component state and sidebar items; static route strings do not prove a React Router mount. The Student Overview embeds the current collaboration card. The active evaluator is `StudentEvaluatorPortal`. The backend `get_current_user`, `require_roles`, origin checks, password/session helpers, database/configuration and health/readiness endpoints are protected.

## Important retained candidates
| Candidate | Why it looks unused | Verification | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| `backend/app/team_compatibility.py` | Not imported by `app.main`. | Imported by `backend/tests/test_team_compatibility.py`; implements shared deterministic compatibility calculations. | Keep: current tests/shared utility explicitly protected. |
| `backend/app/agent_supervision.py` and `backend/app/group_ai.py` | Some corresponding navigation/UI was removed. | Routers are still registered by `app.main`; models/utilities and current tests reference them. | Keep: active API contracts cannot be removed under this task. |
| `backend/app/module2_collaboration.py`, `group_channels.py`, `collaboration_channels.py` | Similar-sounding collaboration implementations. | Different route namespaces/stores; private assignment channels are used by current Student and Supervisor pages. All routers are registered. | Keep; no replacement architecture or route consolidation. |
| `backend/app/models/*`, `backend/app/schemas/*` | Some types lack direct browser usage. | ORM registry, response/body schemas and transitive route dependencies inspected. | Keep: no proven orphan. |
| `backend/app/**/__init__.py` | No explicit caller may name the file itself. | Python package initialization and model re-exports are implicit dependencies. | Keep. |
| `backend/backend/` | Nested directory resembles an accidental copy. | Directory inspected recursively: no files. | Nothing to quarantine/delete. |
| `backend/backups/`, tracked `backend/storage/` files | Not Python imports. | Historical backup/upload data; not executable orphan code. | Keep; data deletion is outside this task. |

## Validation
Validation runs and limitations are recorded in [validation.md](docs/backend-audit/validation.md). Baseline failures must be distinguished from cleanup regressions. No quarantined file may be permanently deleted while any required check fails.

## Final removal list
**None.** No file satisfies the strict confirmed-orphan criteria. No API, schema, model, migration, test, authentication dependency or frontend behavior has been changed.
